It just feels like proof that I'm useless now. Like I was saying before about the fishing and the shop... I just don't get anything out of them anymore. It's just... nothing.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel much pleasure when you're with your family or friends?\n\nPatient: ...I mean, like I said a minute ago about my kids. Even with them, it's just... going through the motions. I don't really feel it. I just stopped caring about all of it, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel sad most of the time?\n\nPatient: ...Didn't you just ask me that? I... I don't really know. Like I said, I wouldn't exactly call it sad. It's just... I don't know. Just empty, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel any pleasure at all in your daily life?\n\nPatient: I... look, I feel like we just went over this. No. I don't. Like I keep saying, I just stopped caring about everything I used to look forward to. \n\nMy fishing gear is just sitting out in the garage untouched. Going out to the woodshop just feels like a chore now. And even when the kids try to get me to play chess or whatever... I'm just going through the motions. There's no pleasure in any of it.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel hopeless?\n\nPatient: ...I don't really know. I haven't thought about it like that. I wouldn't say hopeless, exactly. Just... I don't know. Like I keep saying, just empty.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this way?\n\nPatient: ...A while. I guess... maybe a year and seven months? Give or take. \n\nIt started back when... well, my wife started having some health problems. Everything at home just... changed. So, yeah. Since then.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost a significant amount of weight over the past year and seven months?\n\nPatient: ...I suppose so. I've had to bring my belt in a couple notches. Wasn't really trying to. \n\nJust... most days food tastes like cardboard. I usually skip breakfast, and... even if I've been out on a job site all day, I just kind of pick at dinner. I just don't really care about eating.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts about hurting yourself?\n\nPatient: ...I wouldn't say I want to hurt myself. Not exactly. But... I don't know. \n\nI have to drive out to the sites pretty early, before anyone else is really awake. And... sometimes I just catch myself thinking it would be easier if I just didn't make it there. Just... I don't know. Sometimes the thought of just steering off the road... it scares me a bit. Just because it feels a little too close.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself moving more slowly than you used to or feeling restless and unable to sit still?\n\nPatient: ...I don't really know. I haven't noticed anything like that. I just sort of... get through the day. I don't think I'm moving any slower than usual. And I'm not restless, no.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel worthless or guilty about things?\n\nPatient: ...I suppose. I just... I've been feeling like I'm failing everybody lately. My wife, the kids... the guys on the crew. \n\nIt's just... if anything goes wrong at work, like a cost overrun or some safety issue, I just... I take it pretty hard. It just feels like proof that I'm useless now. Like they'd all be better off if someone else was in charge.\n\nDoctor: Do you often think about death?\n\nPatient: ...I think I already said this, but... yeah. \n\nLike I mentioned before, on those early morning drives to the site... I just think about not making it. Just... steering off the road. It's just... it's a thought that comes up. It scares me sometimes, how close it feels. But... yeah. I guess I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're able to concentrate on things as well as you used to?\n\nPatient: ...No. Not really. \n\nI used to... I mean, I used to be able to keep all the schedules and subcontractors straight in my head. No problem. But now... I don't know. I'll just sit there and read the same email three times. Or I'll be in a meeting and just... forget what I was about to say. \n\nIt's hard. Even small decisions... things I used to figure out in five minutes... I just freeze up. I can't seem to focus on it.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any periods of improvement in your mood over the past year and seven months?\n\nPatient: ...No. Not really. It's just been... pretty much the same.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where your symptoms were worse than they are now?\n\nPatient: ...I don't know. Not that I can remember. \n\nIt just... it all feels about the same. Just heavy. I haven't really noticed it being worse.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Even with them, it's just... going through the motions. I don't really feel it. Like I keep saying, I just stopped caring about everything I used to look forward to. \n\nMy fishing gear is just sitting out in the garage untouched. Like I keep saying, just empty.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this way?\n\nPatient: ...A while. I guess... maybe a year and seven months? Since then.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost a significant amount of weight over the past year and seven months?\n\nPatient: ...I suppose so. I've had to bring my belt in a couple notches. I don't know. \n\nI have to drive out to the sites pretty early, before anyone else is really awake. And... sometimes I just catch myself thinking it would be easier if I just didn't make it there.
